Need implement `_c5_manual(h)` fast:
```
def _c5(h):
    n=len(h)
    dx=2.0/n
    r=np.correlate(h,h,'valid')
    total=h.sum()
    # if total not target, we still objective? Use as is.
    cmax=total - r[0]
    cs=np.cumsum(h)
    # e from1 to n-1
    # S_pre[e] = cs[n-1-e] (if n-1-e index)
    # S_suf[e] = total - (cs[e-1] if e>0 else 0)
    for e in range(1,n):
       sp=cs[n-1-e]
       ss=total - cs[e-1]
       val=sp - r[e]
       if val>cmax: cmax=val
       val=ss - r[e]
       if val>cmax: cmax=val
    return cmax*dx
```
Loop O(n) after correlate; okay. But for many evals (line search), n loop plus correlate C. Can vectorize:
```

sp=cs[:-1]? Need align. cs length n; S_pre[e]=sum h[0:n-e] = cs[n-e-1] for e=1..n-1 => cs[n-2::-1]? Let's do arrays:
L = np.arange(n-1,0,-1) # n-1? Actually e=1 => index n-2; e=n-1=>0. sp=cs[n-2::-1]
ss=total - cs[e-1]; for e array 1..n-1: cs[e-1].
pp=sp - r[1:]
nn=ss - r[1:]
cmax=max(c0, np.max(pp), np.max(nn) if n>1)
```

```

```
def _pgloop(h, n, rng, max_iter, beta0, beta1, lr0=0.05, mu=0.0, noise=0.0):
    h = _proj(h,n)
    f = _c5(h)
    v = np.zeros(n)
    beta = beta0
    no_improve=0
    for it in range(max_iter):
       if _tl()<5: break
       beta = beta0 + (beta1-beta0)*it/max_iter (or schedule)
       g = _smooth_jac(h,n,beta)
       # gradient of C; norm
       norm=np.linalg.norm(g)
       if norm < 1e-12: break
       if mu>0: v = mu*v + g; else v=g
       # adaptive lr = lr0 / (1+norm?) maybe lr0 / sqrt? Since v norm grows
       # Try steps
       moved=False
       # base step scale
       scale = lr0 / (np.linalg.norm(v)+1e-12) * 10? 
       # We can try fixed absolute steps [0.1,0.05,...] independent of n because C units.
       for a in (0.1,0.05,0.025,0.01,0.005,0.02,0.075,0.002):
           h2 = _proj(h - a*v, n)
           f2 = _c5(h2)
           if f2 < f - 1e-15:
               h,f=h2,f2; moved=True; break
       if not moved:
           # try normalized direction with varied steps
           vn = np.linalg.norm(v)
           if vn>1e-12:
              for a in (0.2,0.1,0.05,0.02):
                 h2=_proj(h - a*v/vn,n); f2=...
           if no: # noise
              h2 = _proj(h + 0.01*(rng.randn(n)-0.5), n); f2=...
              if f2 < f + 0.000? accept? maybe only if no improve -> maybe allow increase to escape? risk.
```
Need accept small increases? For escaping local, simulated annealing maybe. But objective hard; random increase could hurt but later recover. Could use limited annealing: accept if f2 < best*1+ temp. But keep global best separate. Better: maintain best copy; while current may increase, if too bad restart from best. This can escape.

```
def _fourier_init(n, rng):
    x=(np.arange(n)+0.5)*2/n
    f=0.0
    for m in [1,2,3,4,5]:
       phi=rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)
       amp=rng.uniform(0,0.3)
       f += amp*np.cos(np.pi*m*x + phi)
    h=0.5+0.5*np.tanh(f) or clip; project
```

Now, exact projection. Implement `_proj(y, n)` robust:
```
def _proj(y, n):
    target=0.5*n
    h=np.array(y,dtype=np.float64)
    h=np.clip(h,0.0,1.0)
    # quick adjust
    for _ in range(10):
       s=h.sum()
       d=target-s
       if abs(d)<1e-13: return h
       free=(h>1e-14)&(h<1-1e-14)
       k=free.sum()
       if k>0:
          h[free]+=d/k
          h=np.clip(h,0,1)
       else:
          # all saturated, binary search
          break
    if abs(h.sum()-target)>1e-10:
       # binary search lambda
       lo=float(h.min())-1.0; hi=float(h.max())+1.0? We need solve sum(clip(y - lam,0,1))=target using original y not current? Use y_clipped? Better original y before clipping. We have y (not modified if we copy). Use y0 = np.asarray.

       # find lam such that x=clip(y0-lam). sum decreases with lam.

       hi=float(np.max(y0))+1.0
       # as lam increases, sum decreases. We want target.
       # if sum at lo < target? set lo lower? y0-lam large -> sum n if lam < min-1. If target maybe n/2. If sum(lo) < target, lower lo more.

       # adjust tiny

       h=x
    return h
```

`_c_vals` fast vectorized:
```
def _c_values(h):
    n=len(h); total=h.sum(); r=np.correlate(h,h,'valid')
    c0=total-r[0]
    if n==1: return np.array([c0])
    cs=np.cumsum(h)
    e_idx=np.arange(1,n)
    sp=cs[n-2::-1] # length n-1
    ss=total - cs[e_idx-1]
    P=sp - r[1:]
    N=ss - r[1:]
    vals=np.empty(2*n-1)
    vals[0]=c0; vals[1:n]=P; vals[n:]=N
    return vals
```
Check `cs[n-2::-1]`: for n=4, cs indices 0,1,2,3. e=1 sp h0..2 index2; e=2 index1; e=3 index0 => [cs[2],cs[1],cs[0]]; `cs[n-2::-1]` = cs[2::-1] -> [2,1,0]. good.
`cs[e_idx-1]` e_idx[0]=1 ->0; good.
Potential tiny negative c due roundoff; objective max unaffected. We can clip vals to 0? Not necessary but for softmax, negative okay. Could do `vals[np.abs(vals)<1e-15]=0`.

`_weights_to_grad` using vectorized formula:
```
def _grad_from_weights(h,n,w):
    n=len(h)
    g=np.zeros(n)
    w0 = w[0] if n>0 else 0
    if w0 !=0: g += w0*(1-2*h)
    if n>1:
       wp=w[1:n]
       wn=w[n:2*n-1]
       gp = np.zeros(n); gn = np.zeros(n)
       if np.any(wp):
          pref=np.cumsum(wp)
          gp[:n-1] = pref[::-1]
       if np.any(wn):
          pref=np.cumsum(wn)
          gn[1:] = pref
       g += gp + gn
       tot=wp+wn
       if np.any(tot):
          A=np.zeros(n); A[1:]=tot
          # product part
          # To reduce allocations, use correlate and convolve
          s1=np.correlate(h, A, 'full')
          # Need slice? s1 index n-1 to 2n-2 inclusive length n
          s2=np.convolve(h, A, 'full')
          g -= s1[n-1:2*n-1] + s2[:n]
    return g
```
Need verify `s1` length 2n-1; slice `n-1 : 2*n-1` (stop exclusive) length n. For n=2, s1[1:3]. good.
If tot all zero, skip.
SLSQP objective gradient of f=dx*(...); this returns dx*g.
We can optionally scale gradient? SLSQP handles.

But `np.correlate(h,A,'full')` for A with zeros may use direct; ok.

def _pgrefine(h,n,rng,iters,beta,lr,mu,return_best=True):
   h=_proj(h,n); f=_c5_exact(h); best=h.copy(); bf=f
   v=np.zeros(n)
   stuck=0
   for it in range(iters):
      if _tl()<5: break
      g=_smooth_jac(h,n,beta)
      gn=np.linalg.norm(g)
      if gn<1e-14: break
      v = mu*v + (1.0-mu)*g? Momentum formula usually v=mu*v+g. Use v=mu*v+g.
      vn=np.linalg.norm(v)
      if vn<1e-14: v=g; vn=gn
      # step sizes relative to 1/vn to limit change ~lr
      # We want h change ~a (C values units). direction unit = v/vn. Try a in [0.2,0.1,...] on unit direction.
      tried=[]
      for a in (0.2,0.1,0.05,0.025,0.01,0.005,0.02,0.075,0.002):
          h2=_proj(h - (a/vn)*v, n)
          f2=_c5_exact(h2)
          if f2 < f - 1e-15:
             h=f2; break
          # Also if f2 < bf - 1e-15 but not < f? can't since f maybe current; no.
      else:
         # try non-normalized? maybe direction gradient scale; unit ok.
         # If no improvement, reduce momentum and perturb
         if stuck > 20:
            # restart from best with noise
            h2=_proj(best + 0.005*rng.randn(n) - 0.0025, n) # keep mean? proj handles
            f2=_c5_exact(h2)
            if f2 < bf + 0.0002: h=h2; f=f2
            stuck=0
         stuck+=1
         # try active gradient? maybe change beta? beta fixed per call.
         v=0.5*v
   if f<bf: best...
   return best,bf
```

x=(np.arange(n)+0.5)*2/n
dev=np.zeros(n)
for m in range(1,K+1):
   amp=rng? in param
   phi
   dev += amp * np.cos(np.pi*m*x + phi)
# scale to ensure |dev|<=0.5*0.95? leave margin
maxabs=np.max(np.abs(dev))
if maxabs>1e-12: dev *= min(1.0, 0.5*0.98/maxabs)
h=0.5+dev
h=_proj(h,n)
```

Detailed `_greedy_step`:
```
def _try_step(h, f, g, n, steps):
   ng=np.linalg.norm(g)
   if ng<1e-14: return h,f,False
   d = g/ng
   best_h=h; best_f=f
   for a in steps:
      h2=_proj(h - a*d, n)
      f2=_c5_exact(h2)
      if f2 < best_f - 1e-15:

`_pgrefine` pseudo:
```
def _pgrefine(h,n,rng,iters,beta,lr_steps=(0.4,0.2,0.1,0.05,0.025,0.01,0.005),mu=0.0,noise_amp=0.0):
  h=_proj(h,n); f=_c5_exact(h); best=h.copy(); bf=f
  v=np.zeros(n); stagn=0
  for it in range(iters):
     if _tl()<5: break
     g=_smooth_jac(h,n,beta)
     # occasionally active grad if beta==1e6? We pass beta; can call _active_jac if beta large.
     if beta >= 1e5: g = _active_jac(h,n,1e-9/dx? but _smooth_jac handles)
     gv=np.linalg.norm(g)
     if gv<1e-14: break
     if mu>0: v = mu*v + g else v=g
     vn = np.linalg.norm(v)
     if vn<1e-14: v=g; vn=gv
     d = v/vn
     best_step_f=f; best_h=h
     for a in lr_steps:
        h2=_proj(h - a*d, n)
        f2=_c5_exact(h2)
        if f2 < best_step_f - 1e-15:
            best_step_h=h2; best_step_f=f2
     if best_step_f < f:
        h,f=best_step_h,best_step_f
        if f < bf - 1e-15: best,bf=h.copy(),f; stagn=0
        else: stagn=1
     else:
        stagn +=1
        # try smaller or random direction?
        if stagn > 10:
           # add noise to direction
           r = rng.randn(n); r -= r.mean(); r /= (np.linalg.norm(r)+1e-12)
           mixed = 0.8*d + 0.2*r
           mixed /= (np.linalg.norm(mixed)+1e-12)
           for a in (0.05,0.02,0.01):
              h2=_proj(h - a*mixed,n); f2=...
              if f2 < f: ...
           if no:
              if noise_amp>0:
                 h2=_proj(h + noise_amp*(rng.randn(n)); f2 ... if f2 < f + 0.0001 maybe reset current? 
              stagn=0; v=0.5*v
        else:
           v=0.5*v
  return best,bf
```
Since line search tries fixed steps, sometimes best improvement very small. We can accept if f2 < f - 1e-13. If none, stagnation.
Momentum direction can conflict with constraint projection; okay.
No increase acceptance might get stuck at flat. But local search and restarts can escape. We can keep global best and if stagnation high, restart from best with noise:
```
if stagn>30:
    h2=_proj(best + 0.01*rng.randn(n),n)
    f2=_c5_exact(h2)
    if f2 < bf + 0.0005: h=h2; f=f2; v=0; stagn=0
```
This may recover.

SLSQP implementation:
```
_SG_N=0; _SG_BETA=100.0
def _slq_obj(x): return _soft_value(x,_SG_N,_SG_BETA)
def _slq_jac(x): return _soft_jac_only(x,_SG_N,_SG_BETA)
def _slq_con(x): return x.sum()-0.5*_SG_N
def _slq_con_jac(x): return np.ones(_SG_N)
def _slsqp(h,n,beta,maxiter):
   global _SG_N,_SG_BETA
   _SG_N=n; _SG_BETA=beta
   h0=_proj(h,n)
   try:
      cons=[{'type':'eq','fun':_slq_con,'jac':_slq_con_jac}]
      bounds=[(0,1)]*n
      res=minimize(_slq_obj,h0,jac=_slq_jac,method='SLSQP',bounds=bounds,cons=cons,options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-13,'iprint':0})
      x=_proj(np.asarray(res.x),n)
   except: x=h0
   return x
```
Soft value and jac: `_soft_jac_only` returns gradient C. `_soft_value` can compute vals, beta, mx. If beta>1e4, value=mx*dx (non-smooth) but jac active; SLSQP may not like kink. Use beta finite for SLSQP (e.g., 200,1000) not active.
To avoid repeated objective value computation cost, okay.

But SLSQP with equality and bounds may be slow if n=256. Use if time > maybe 120s and n<=220. We can call maxiter 80. It may improve.

Local polish function details:
```
def _local_polish(h,n,rng,sweeps=20,pair_trials=40):
   h=_proj(h,n); f=_c5_exact(h); best=h.copy(); bf=f
   for sweep in range(sweeps):
      if _tl()<3: break
      improved=False
      hi=np.where(h>0.7)[0]; lo=np.where(h<0.3)[0]
      mid=np.where((h>=0.3)&(h<=0.7))[0]
      for _ in range(pair_trials):
         if _tl()<2: break
         # choose pair
         if len(hi)>0 and len(lo)>0 and rng.rand()<0.7:
            i=hi[rng.randint(len(hi))]; j=lo[rng.randint(len(lo))]
         elif len(mid)>0:
            i=mid[rng.randint(len(mid))]; j=mid[rng.randint(len(mid))]
         else: i,j=rng.randint(n),rng.randint(n)
         if i==j: continue
         # generate candidate deltas
         vals = best[i],best[j]
         # Try swap and transfers
         candidates=[]
         if vals[0] != vals[1]:
            # transfers from higher to lower
            diff=abs(vals[0]-vals[1])
            for frac in (0.5,0.25,0.1,0.75,0.03):
               t=frac*diff
               # direction: move from high to low (reduce spread)
               # if hi>lo, i-=t,j+=t; else swap
               candidates.append(t with sign)
         for sign in (1,-1):
            h2=best.copy()
            # swap
            h2[i],h2[j]=best[j],best[i]
            f2=_c5_exact(h2)
            if f2<bf-1e-14: update
         for t in deltas: # small random
            h2=best.copy(); h2[i]+=t; h2[j]-=t; h2=_proj(h2,n); f2=...
      if not improved: maybe try random pair all fractions
   return best,bf
```
But full `_c5_exact` per candidate. Could be okay if limited 1000 calls per n.
Maybe better pair optimization: For chosen pair, evaluate delta by scanning a few values without projecting each? If adjusting sum, projection not needed if t within bounds and sum preserved. For transfer t from i to j: ensure 0≤h_i±t≤1. Sum preserved. Try t values. For swap, sum preserved. Use no projection.
We'll implement:
- Determine max transfer tmax = min(max(0, hi-lo)/2? Actually moving both toward average: if a>b, can transfer s from i to j up to (a-b)/2 to equalize, or more to overshoot. Try s in [0.01,0.05,0.1,0.25*(a-b),0.5*(a-b),0.75*(a-b),(a-b)] if within.
Simpler: If a>b, try new_i = a - t, new_j = b + t for t in [0.005,0.01,0.02,0.05,0.1, (a-b)*0.5, (a-b)*0.25, (a-b)*0.75, max(a-0,1-b)] bounded. Also reverse (increase spread) maybe if improves? Try ± for random.
Update only if f2<bf.

Implement `_fourier_h(p,n,K)`:
```
def _fourier_h(p,n,K):
    x=(np.arange(n)+0.5)*2.0/n
    u=np.zeros(n)
    idx=0
    for m in range(1,K+1):
       amp=p[idx]; phi=p[idx+1]; idx+=2
       if abs(amp)>1e-12:
           u += amp * np.cos(np.pi*m*x + phi)
    h=0.5 + 0.5*np.tanh(u)
    return _proj(h,n)
```
Tanh ensures bounds but projection. The mean of tanh(u) may not zero; projection. Good.

def _soft_jac(h,n,beta):
   dx=2.0/n
   vals=_c_values(h)
   mx=float(np.max(vals))
   if beta > 1e5:
      tol = max(1e-10, 1e-9/dx)  # raw? C tol 1e-9? Maybe too small; use 1e-7/dx? 
      act = vals >= mx - tol
      if not np.any(act): act = vals == mx? use argmax
      w=np.zeros_like(vals); w[act]=1.0/np.count_nonzero(act)
      return dx*_grad_from_weights(h,n,w)
   z=np.exp(beta*(vals-mx)); s=z.sum(); if s<=0... active max
   w=z/s
   return dx*_grad_from_weights(h,n,w)
```

Implementation:
```
def _proj(y,n):
    target=0.5*n
    h=np.array(y,dtype=np.float64)
    h=np.clip(h,0.0,1.0)
    for _ in range(8):
       s=h.sum(); d=target-s
       if abs(d)<1e-13: break
       free=(h>1e-14)&(h<1.0-1e-14)
       kf=int(free.sum())
       if kf>0:
          h[free]+=d/kf
          np.clip(h,0.0,1.0,out=h)
       else:
          break
    if abs(h.sum()-target)>1e-10:
       y0=np.clip(y,0.0,1.0)? Actually projection onto sum of y not clipped? The unconstrained projection uses original y, not clipped? Since box constraints: x=clip(y-λ), yes y original. But y may have out of bounds; using y original is correct. We used quick h but fallback `y0 = np.asarray(y,dtype=float)`.
       y0=np.clip(y0,-1e6,1e6)
       lo=float(np.min(y0))-1.0
       hi=float(np.max(y0))+1.0
       for _ in range(60):
          mid=0.5*(lo+hi)
          x=np.clip(y0-mid,0.0,1.0)
          s=float(x.sum())
          if s>target:
             lo=mid
          else:
             hi=mid
       x=np.clip(y0-0.5*(lo+hi),0.0,1.0)
       # adjust residual
       rem=target-float(x.sum())
       if abs(rem)>0:
          free=(x>1e-14)&(x<1.0-1e-14)
          kf=int(free.sum())
          if kf>0: x[free]+=rem/kf; np.clip(x,0,1,out=x)
          else: x[:] = target/n
       h=x
    # exact sum
    rem=target-float(h.sum())
    if abs(rem)>1e-15:
       free=(h>1e-14)&(h<1-1e-14); k=int(free.sum())
       if k>0: h[free]+=rem/k
       else:
          # perturb smallest? set all 0.5?
          h[:]=0.5
       np.clip(h,0,1,out=h)
    return h
```

x=np.arange(n)
v=np.mod(x*alpha,1.0)
h=(v<0.5).astype(float)
# fix sum: target=n/2; if sum>target, change largest v? Need top ones.
s=h.sum(); target=0.5*n
if s>target:
   # zero out ones with largest v
   ones=np.where(h>0.5)[0]
   ones=ones[np.argsort(v[ones])][::-1]
   h[ones[:int(s-target)]] =0
elif s<target:
   zeros=np.where(h<0.5)[0]
   zeros=zeros[np.argsort(v[zeros])]
   h[zeros[:int(target-s)]]=1
return _proj(h,n)

```
def _polish(h,n,rng,quick=False):
   h=_proj(h,n); f=_c5_exact(h); best=h.copy(); bf=f
   # iters
   base= max(40, int(12000.0/n))
   if quick:
      base=max(20, base//3)
   # PG
   for beta, mult in ((20,1.0),(80,1.0),(300,1.0),(1000,0.7),(1e6,0.5)):
      iters=int(base*mult)
      if iters<10: continue
      h2,f2=_pgrefine(best,n,rng,iters,beta,mu=0.4 if beta<1000 else 0.2,step=0.15)
      if f2<bf: best,bf=h2,f2
      # local occasionally
   if not quick: _local_polish(20,80)
   else: _local_polish(5,30)
   # SLSQP if n<=300
   if not quick and n<=300 and _left()>20:
      for beta in (300,1200):
         h2=_slq_improve(best,n,beta, maxiter=(80 if n<=180 else 40))
         f2=_c5_exact(h2); update
         # quick pg after
   # final active
   h2,f2=_pgrefine(best,n,rng,int(base*0.6),1e6,mu=0.0,step=0.05)
   update
   local
   return best,bf
```

Implementation details:
```
def _pgrefine(h,n,rng,iters,beta,mu,step):
   h=_proj(h,n); f=_c5_exact(h); best=h.copy(); bf=f
   v=np.zeros(n)
   stagn=0
   for it in range(iters):
      if _left()<3: break
      g=_soft_jac(h,n,beta)
      if not np.all(np.isfinite(g)): break
      gn=np.linalg.norm(g)
      if gn<1e-14: break
      if mu>0:
         v = mu*v + g
      else:
         v = g
      vn=np.linalg.norm(v)
      if vn<1e-14: v=g; vn=gn
      d=v/vn
      improved=False
      # try factors, starting with step; maybe step adaptive
      for factor in (1.0,0.5,0.25,0.1,0.05,0.02,0.01):
         a=step*factor
         if a<=1e-6: break
         h2=_proj(h - a*d, n)
         f2=_c5_exact(h2)
         if f2 < f - 1e-14:
             h=f2; step=min(0.5,a*1.1); improved=True
             break
      if improved:
         if f < bf - 1e-14: best=h.copy(); bf=f; stagn=0
         else: stagn=1
      else:
         step=max(1e-4,step*0.5)
         stagn+=1
         v=0.5*v
         if stagn>20:
             # try random restart/noise
             if rng is not None:
                nz=0.01*np.sqrt(n?)? Choose L2 noise 0.1? Per coord 0.01.
                h2=_proj(h + 0.01*(rng.standard_normal(n) - 0.5), n)
                f2=_c5_exact(h2)
                if f2 < f:  # only accept decrease
                   h,f=h2,f2
                # also if f2 < bf + 0.0005 maybe reset current to it (increase current but not best)
                elif f2 < bf + 0.0005:
                   h,f=h2,f2
                v=np.zeros(n)
             stagn=0
   return best,bf
```
But `f` variable used incorrectly: after accept, set `h,f=h2,f2`. In code use `h = h2; f = f2`.
Noise increases current may plateau; best preserved.
Maybe accept increase to escape if lower than best+0.0002. Good.

Step initial: 0.15 for normal, 0.05 active. Since direction L2 unit, step 0.15 yields per coord ~0.01; safe. Could be too small for large improvements; if accepted, step increases by 1.1 capped 0.5. Good.

Momentum: v is gradient C units maybe norm ~1? Norm can be 0.1-10. d unit. Step independent of gradient norm. Maybe if gradient small, direction unreliable; but stagnation handles.

`_local_polish` implementation more concrete:
```
def _local_polish(h,n,rng,sweeps,trials):
   h=_proj(h,n); f=_c5_exact(h); best=h.copy(); bf=f
   for sweep in range(sweeps):
      if _left()<2: break
      improved=False
      for _ in range(trials):
         if _left()<1: break
         i=rng.randint(n); j=rng.randint(n)
         if i==j: continue
         a=best[i]; b=best[j]
         if a==b: continue
         # swap
         h2=best.copy(); h2[i]=b; h2[j]=a; f2=_c5_exact(h2)
         if f2<bf-1e-14: update best/f; improved=True; continue
         # transfer towards equal
         if a > b:
             hi,lo=i,j
         else: hi,lo=j,i
         diff=best[hi]-best[lo]
         maxmove=min(diff, 1.0-best[lo], best[hi])
         if maxmove>1e-9:
            fracs=(0.25,0.5,0.75,0.1,0.9,0.03,0.4)
            for fr in fracs:
                t=fr*maxmove
                h2=best.copy(); h2[hi]=best[hi]-t; h2[lo]=best[lo]+t
                # if want also reverse (increase difference): maybe try -t?
                f2=_c5_exact(h2)
                if f2<bf-1e-14: update; improved=True; break
            # reverse small if no improve:
            for fr in (0.1,0.3):
                t=fr*maxmove
                # move from lo to hi (increase spread)
                tmax2=min(best[lo], 1-best[hi])
                if t > tmax2: t=tmax2*0.5
                if t>1e-9:
                   h2=best.copy(); h2[lo]=best[lo]-t; h2[hi]=best[hi]+t; check
         # random small coordinate delta to nearby partner
         t=0.02*rng.choice([-1,1])
         if 0<=best[i]+t<=1 and 0<=best[j]-t<=1:
             h2=best.copy(); h2[i]+=t; h2[j]-=t; check
      if not improved and _left()<2: break
   return best,bf
```
This may be okay. Use update function repeated; define helper `_try_update`? Cannot closure; implement inline.
Need avoid too many copies. Could allocate h2=best.copy() each candidate. Fine.

`_slq_improve`:
```
def _slq_improve(h,n,beta,maxiter):
   if _left()<20 or n>400: return _proj(h,n)
   global _SG_N,_SG_BETA
   _SG_N=n; _SG_BETA=beta
   h0=_proj(h,n)
   try:
      bounds=[(0.0,1.0) for _ in range(n)] # this uses comprehension top-level okay? It's not lambda/closure.
      cons=[{'type':'eq','fun':_slq_con,'jac':_slq_con_jac}]
      res=minimize(_slq_obj,h0,jac=_slq_jac,method='SLSQP',bounds=bounds,constraints=cons,options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-13,'iprint':0})
      np.asarray(res.x)
   except Exception:
      x=h0
   return _proj(x,n)
```

Potential issue: `minimize` with SLSQP does not accept `jac` for constraint if provided? It does accept `jac` in constraint dict? In scipy, constraint dict can have 'jac' for SLSQP? Yes I think. If not, catch? Could omit to be safe. Constraint finite diff cheap (1 variable). Omit jac to avoid compatibility. Use `constraints=[{'type':'eq','fun':_slq_con}]`.
SLSQP may call objective with x sum not exact; our soft value uses h as is, no projection. It can handle. But if x outside bounds slightly, clip? It may. We can in `_slq_obj` project? If project inside objective, discontinuity? But SLSQP bounds keep. Maybe project to ensure finite: `x=np.clip(x,0,1)`. It violates constraint slightly; SLSQP penalizes. Instead don't project, just clip for correlation? If outside small, correlation can negative products? h may negative if SLSQP tries; objective maybe unnatural. Use `h=np.clip(h,0,1)` inside objective/jac. That makes projection approximate. But gradient of clipped objective not exact at bounds; okay with bounds. Implement helper `_clipped(h,n)`: clip.

`_c5_manual` code:
```
def _c5_manual(h):
    n=len(h)
    if n==0: return 0.0
    r=np.correlate(h,h,'full')[n-1:]
    total=float(h.sum())
    mx=total - r[0]
    if n>1:
       cs=np.cumsum(h)
       sp=cs[n-2::-1]
       ss=total - cs[:n-1]
       pp=sp - r[1:]
       nn=ss - r[1:]
       v=float(np.max(pp)); if v>mx: mx=v
       v=float(np.max(nn)); if v>mx: mx=v
    return float(mx)*(2.0/n)
```
Check `ss=total-cs[:n-1]`: e=1 suffix index1 sum total-h0; e=n-1 suffix h[n-1] total-cs[n-2]. yes.
But for e=n-1, sp=cs[0]=h0; r[n-1] maybe 0? Good.

def _single_sine_start(n,rng):
   best_h=None; best_c=np.inf
   for a in (0.18,0.22,0.26,0.30,0.34,0.38,0.42):
      for ph in np.linspace(0.0,2*np.pi,17,endpoint=False):
         p=np.array([a,ph],float)
         h=_fourier_h(p,n,1)
         c=_c5_manual(h)
         if c<best_c: best_h=h.copy(); best_c=c
   return best_h,best_c
```

def _interp_h(h, ns, nd):
    h=np.asarray(h,float)
    if ns==nd: return _proj(h,nd)
    xs=(np.arange(ns)+0.5)*(2.0/ns)
    xd=(np.arange(nd)+0.5)*(2.0/nd)
    return _proj(np.interp(xd,xs,h),nd)
```

```
def _grad_from_weights(h,n,w):
    g=np.zeros(n,float)
    if n==0: return g
    w=float(w[0])
    if w !=0.0: g += w*(1.0-2.0*h)
    if n>1:
       wp=w[1:n]
       wn=w[n:2*n-1]
       if wp.sum() !=0? Floating sum maybe. Use if np.any(wp !=0).
       ...
```
For direct, if wp all zero, skip. But `np.cumsum(wp)` on zeros fine. Use unconditional to simplify.
```
pref_wp=np.cumsum(wp)
gp=np.zeros(n)
gp[:n-1]=pref_wp[::-1]
g += gp
pref_wn=np.cumsum(wn)
gn=np.zeros(n)
gn[1:]=pref_wn
g += gn
tot=wp+wn
A=np.zeros(n); A[1:]=tot
if np.any(tot):
   s1=np.correlate(h,A,'full')[n-1:2*n-1]
   s2=np.convolve(h,A,'full')[:n]
   g -= (s1+s2)
return g
```
Need ensure w length 2n-1; for n=1 w[1:n] empty, skip.

`_soft_value`:
```
def _soft_value(h,n,beta):
   vals=_c_values(h)
   mx=float(np.max(vals))
   if beta <= 0: return mx*(2.0/n)
   if beta > 1e5: return mx*(2.0/n)
   z=np.exp(beta*(vals-mx))
   s=float(z.sum())
   if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
       return mx*(2.0/n)
   return (mx + math.log(s)/beta)*(2.0/n)
```
If vals-mx large negative and beta high, underflow s=1 at max, log 0, good. Use `math.log`.

```
def _soft_jac(h,n,beta):
   vals=_c_values(h); mx=np.max(vals); dx=2.0/n
   if beta > 1e5:
      tol = max(1e-8, 1e-7/dx)
      act = vals >= (mx - tol)
      if not np.any(act):
         act = (vals == mx)
      if not np.any(act):
         w=np.zeros_like(vals); w[int(np.argmax(vals))]=1.0
      else:
         w=np.zeros_like(vals); w[act]=1.0/np.count_nonzero(act)
      return dx*_grad_from_weights(h,n,w)
   z=...
   if s...: active max
   else: w=z/s
   return dx*_grad_from_weights(h,n,w)
```

`_slq_obj`, `_slq_jac`, `_slq_con`:
```
def _slq_obj(x):
    h=np.clip(np.asarray(x,dtype=np.float64),0.0,1.0)
    return _soft_value(h,_SG_N,_SG_BETA)
def _slq_jac(x):
    h=np.clip(np.asarray(x,dtype=np.float64),0.0,1.0)
    return _soft_jac(h,_SG_N,_SG_BETA)
def _slq_con(x):
    return float(np.sum(x) - 0.5*_SG_N)
```
If x length not _SG_N? okay.

`_slq_improve`:
```
def _slq_improve(h,n,beta,maxiter):
    if _left()<45 or n>230: return _proj(h,n)
    global _SG_N,_SG_BETA
    _SG_N=n; _SG_BETA=float(beta)
    h0=_proj(h,n)
    try:
       bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*n
       cons=[{'type':'eq','fun':_slq_con}]
       res=minimize(_slq_obj,h0,jac=_slq_jac,method='SLSQP',bounds=bounds,constraints=cons,options={'maxiter':int(maxiter),'ftol':1e-12,'iprint':0})
       x=np.asarray(res.x,dtype=np.float64)
    except Exception:
       x=h0
    return _proj(x,n)
```

def _rand_params(K,rng):
    amps = np.array([0.7,0.5,0.35,0.25,0.18,0.12])[:K]
    amps = amps * rng.uniform(0.5,1.8,K)
    phases = rng.uniform(-np.pi,np.pi,K)
    return np.concatenate((amps,phases))

```
def _sturmian_h(n, alpha):
    v=np.mod(np.arange(n)*alpha,1.0)
    h=(v<0.5).astype(np.float64)
    s=int(h.sum()); target=0.5*n
    # target may be float; need integer because h binary. We use even n.
    t=int(target)
    if s>t:
       ones=np.where(h>0.5)[0]
       if len(ones)>0:
          order=ones[np.argsort(v[ones])][::-1]
          h[order[:s-t]]=0.0
    elif s<t:
       zeros=np.where(h<0.5)[0]
       if len(zeros)>0:
          order=zeros[np.argsort(v[zeros])]
          h[order[:t-s]]=1.0
    return _proj(h,n)
```
If n even, target integer. If odd? we ensure even.

```
def _default_h(n,rng):
    x=(np.arange(n)+0.5)*2.0/n
    u=0.35*np.cos(np.pi*x+0.3)+0.25*np.cos(2*np.pi*x+1.1)+0.15*np.cos(3*np.pi*x+2.2)
    if rng is not None:
       u += rng.uniform(-0.05,0.05)
    h=0.5+0.5*np.tanh(u)
    return _proj(h,n)
```

def _c5_exact(h):
    n=len(h)
    if n==0: return 0.0
    return float(np.max(np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full')))*(2.0/n)

```python
import time
import math
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize

_t0 = 0.0
_tb = 1300.0
_LOCALEND = None

_FOU_N = 160
_FOU_K = 3

_SG_N = 160
_SG_BETA = 100.0


def _left():
    rem = _tb - (time.time() - _t0) - 3.0
    if _LOCALEND is not None:
        rem = min(rem, _LOCALEND - time.time())
    if rem < 0.0:
        rem = 0.0
    return rem


def _proj(y, n):
    n = int(n)
    target = 0.5 * n
    h = np.asarray(y, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    if h.size != n or not np.all(np.isfinite(h)):
        h = np.full(n, 0.5, dtype=np.float64)
        target = 0.5 * n

    np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0, out=h)

    for _ in range(10):
        s = float(h.sum())
        d = target - s
        if abs(d) < 1e-13:
            break
        free = (h > 1e-14) & (h < 1.0 - 1e-14)
        kf = int(np.count_nonzero(free))
        if kf > 0:
            h[free] += d / kf
            np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0, out=h)
        else:
            break

    if abs(float(h.sum()) - target) > 1e-10:
        y0 = np.asarray(y, dtype=np.float64).copy()
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(y0)):
            y0 = 0.5 * np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)

        lo = float(np.min(y0)) - 2.0
        hi = float(np.max(y0)) + 2.0
        for _ in range(70):
            mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
            x = np.clip(y0 - mid, 0.0, 1.0)
            s = float(x.sum())
            if s > target:
                lo = mid
            else:
                hi = mid

        x = np.clip(y0 - 0.5 * (lo + hi), 0.0, 1.0)
        rem = target - float(x.sum())
        if abs(rem) > 0.0:
            free = (x > 1e-14) & (x < 1.0 - 1e-14)
            kf = int(np.count_nonzero(free))
            if kf > 0:
                x[free] += rem / kf
                np.clip(x, 0.0, 1.0, out=x)
            else:
                x[:] = target / n
        h = x

    rem = target - float(h.sum())
    if abs(rem) > 1e-15:
        free = (h > 1e-14) & (h < 1.0 - 1e-14)
        kf = int(np.count_nonzero(free))
        if kf > 0:
            h[free] += rem / kf
        else:
            h[:] = target / n
        np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0, out=h)

    return h


def _interp_h(h, ns, nd):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=np.float64)
    ns = int(ns)
    nd = int(nd)
    if nd <= 0:
        return h
    if ns == nd:
        return _proj(h, nd)
    xs = (np.arange(ns) + 0.5) * (2.0 / ns)
    xd = (np.arange(nd) + 0.5) * (2.0 / nd)
    return _proj(np.interp(xd, xs, h), nd)


def _c5_exact(h):
    n = int(len(h))
    if n <= 0:
        return 0.0
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
    return float(np.max(c)) * (2.0 / n)


def _c5_manual(h):
    n = int(len(h))
    if n <= 0:
        return 0.0
    r = np.correlate(h, h, mode="full")[n - 1:]
    total = float(h.sum())
    mx = total - float(r[0])
    if n > 1:
        cs = np.cumsum(h)
        sp = cs[n - 2::-1]
        ss = total - cs[:n - 1]
        pp = sp - r[1:]
        nn = ss - r[1:]
        v = float(np.max(pp))
        if v > mx:
            mx = v
        v = float(np.max(nn))
        if v > mx:
            mx = v
    if mx < 0.0:
        mx = 0.0
    return float(mx) * (2.0 / n)


def _c_values(h):
    n = int(len(h))
    if n <= 0:
        return np.zeros(1, dtype=np.float64)

    r = np.correlate(h, h, mode="full")[n - 1:]
    total = float(h.sum())
    vals = np.empty(2 * n - 1, dtype=np.float64)
    vals[0] = total - float(r[0])
    if n > 1:
        cs = np.cumsum(h)
        sp = cs[n - 2::-1]
        ss = total - cs[:n - 1]
        vals[1:n] = sp - r[1:]
        vals[n:2 * n - 1] = ss - r[1:]
    return vals


def _grad_from_weights(h, n, w):
    n = int(n)
    g = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if n <= 0:
        return g

    w0 = float(w[0]) if len(w) > 0 else 0.0
    if w0 != 0.0:
        g += w0 * (1.0 - 2.0 * h)

    if n > 1:
        wp = w[1:n]
        wn = w[n:2 * n - 1]

        if str(wp.dtype)[:2] != "in":
            wp = wp.astype(np.float64)
        if str(wn.dtype)[:2] != "in":
            wn = wn.astype(np.float64)

        pref_wp = np.cumsum(wp)
        g[:n - 1] += pref_wp[::-1]

        pref_wn = np.cumsum(wn)
        g[1:] += pref_wn

        tot = wp + wn
        if np.any(tot != 0.0):
            A = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
            A[1:] = tot
            s1 = np.correlate(h, A, mode="full")[n - 1:2 * n - 1]
            s2 = np.convolve(h, A, mode="full")[:n]
            g -= (s1 + s2)

    return g


def _weights_from_vals(vals, mx, beta):
    vals = np.asarray(vals, dtype=np.float64)
    if beta > 1e5:
        dx = max(1e-12, 2.0 / max(1, int(len(vals) // 2 + 1)))
        tol = max(1e-8, 1e-7 / dx)
        act = vals >= (mx - tol)
        if not np.any(act):
            act = (vals == mx)
        w = np.zeros_like(vals, dtype=np.float64)
        if not np.any(act):
            w[int(np.argmax(vals))] = 1.0
        else:
            w[act] = 1.0 / float(np.count_nonzero(act))
        return w

    z = np.exp(float(beta) * (vals - mx))
    s = float(z.sum())
    w = np.zeros_like(vals, dtype=np.float64)
    if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
        w[int(np.argmax(vals))] = 1.0
    else:
        w = z / s
    return w


def _soft_value(h, n, beta):
    vals = _c_values(h)
    mx = float(np.max(vals))
    dx = 2.0 / int(n)
    if beta <= 0.0 or beta > 1e5:
        return mx * dx
    z = np.exp(float(beta) * (vals - mx))
    s = float(z.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return mx * dx
    return (mx + math.log(s) / float(beta)) * dx


def _soft_jac(h, n, beta):
    vals = _c_values(h)
    mx = float(np.max(vals))
    dx = 2.0 / int(n)
    w = _weights_from_vals(vals, mx, beta)
    return dx * _grad_from_weights(h, n, w)


def _slq_obj(x):
    h = np.clip(np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64), 0.0, 1.0)
    return _soft_value(h, _SG_N, _SG_BETA)


def _slq_jac(x):
    h = np.clip(np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64), 0.0, 1.0)
    return _soft_jac(h, _SG_N, _SG_BETA)


def _slq_con(x):
    return float(np.sum(x) - 0.5 * _SG_N)


def _slq_improve(h, n, beta, maxiter):
    n = int(n)
    if _left() < 45.0 or n > 230:
        return _proj(h, n)

    global _SG_N, _SG_BETA
    _SG_N = n
    _SG_BETA = float(beta)
    h0 = _proj(h, n)
    x = h0
    try:
        bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n
        constraints = [{"type": "eq", "fun": _slq_con}]
        res = minimize(
            _slq_obj,
            h0,
            jac=_slq_jac,
            method="SLSQP",
            bounds=bounds,
            constraints=constraints,
            options={"maxiter": int(maxiter), "ftol": 1e-12, "iprint": 0},
        )
        x = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
    except Exception:
        x = h0
    return _proj(x, n)


def _eval_candidate(h):
    return _c5_manual(h)


def _try_candidate(h, limit):
    f = _eval_candidate(h)
    if f < limit - 1e-14:
        return True, f
    return False, f


def _pgrefine(h, n, rng, iters, beta, mu, step):
    n = int(n)
    h = _proj(h, n)
    f = _c5_manual(h)
    best = h.copy()
    bf = f
    if n <= 1:
        return best, bf

    v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    stagn = 0

    for _ in range(int(iters)):
        if _left() < 4.0:
            break

        g = _soft_jac(h, n, float(beta))
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(g)):
            break
        gn = float(np.linalg.norm(g))
        if gn < 1e-14:
            break

        if mu > 0.0:
            v = float(mu) * v + g
        else:
            v = g

        vn = float(np.linalg.norm(v))
        if vn < 1e-14:
            v = g
            vn = gn
        d = v / vn

        improved = False
        for factor in (1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 0.1, 0.05, 0.02, 0.01):
            a = float(step) * float(factor)
            if a <= 1e-6:
                break
            h2 = _proj(h - a * d, n)
            f2 = _c5_manual(h2)
            if f2 < f - 1e-14:
                h = h2
                f = f2
                step = min(0.6, a * 1.15)
                improved = True
                break

        if improved:
            if f < bf - 1e-14:
                best = h.copy()
                bf = f
                stagn = 0
            else:
                stagn = 1
        else:
            step = max(0.001, float(step) * 0.6)
            stagn += 1
            v *= 0.5

            if stagn > 15 or step <= 0.00101:
                noise = 0.02 * rng.standard_normal(n)
                h2 = _proj(h + noise - 0.02 * float(np.mean(noise)), n)
                f2 = _c5_manual(h2)
                if f2 < f:
                    h = h2
                    f = f2
                elif f2 < bf + 3.0e-4:
                    h = h2
                    f = f2
                v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
                step = 0.1
                stagn = 0

    return best, bf


def _local_polish(h, n, rng, sweeps=20, trials=60):
    n = int(n)
    h = _proj(h, n)
    f = _c5_manual(h)
    best = h.copy()
    bf = f
    if n <= 1:
        return best, bf

    for _ in range(int(sweeps)):
        if _left() < 2.0:
            break
        improved = False

        for _ in range(int(trials)):
            if _left() < 1.0:
                break

            i = int(rng.randint(n))
            j = int(rng.randint(n))
            if i == j:
                continue

            a = float(best[i])
            b = float(best[j])
            if a == b:
                continue

            h2 = best.copy()
            h2[i], h2[j] = b, a
            ok, f2 = _try_candidate(h2, bf)
            if ok:
                best, bf = h2, f2
                improved = True
                continue

            if a > b:
                hi, lo = i, j
            else:
                hi, lo = j, i

            diff = float(best[hi]) - float(best[lo])
            maxmove = min(diff, 1.0 - float(best[lo]), float(best[hi]))
            if maxmove > 1e-10:
                for fr in (0.5, 0.25, 0.75, 0.1, 0.4, 0.03, 0.9):
                    t = float(fr) * maxmove
                    if t < 1e-10:
                        continue
                    h2 = best.copy()
                    h2[hi] = float(best[hi]) - t
                    h2[lo] = float(best[lo]) + t
                    ok, f2 = _try_candidate(h2, bf)
                    if ok:
                        best, bf = h2, f2
                        improved = True
                        break
                if improved:
                    continue

                maxmove2 = min(float(best[lo]), 1.0 - float(best[hi]))
                if maxmove2 > 1e-10:
                    for fr in (0.3, 0.1, 0.5):
                        t = float(fr) * maxmove2
                        if t < 1e-10:
                            continue
                        h2 = best.copy()
                        h2[lo] = float(best[lo]) - t
                        h2[hi] = float(best[hi]) + t
                        ok, f2 = _try_candidate(h2, bf)
                        if ok:
                            best, bf = h2, f2
                            improved = True
                            break
                    if improved:
                        continue

            for t in (0.02, -0.02, 0.01, -0.01):
                ni = float(best[i]) + t
                nj = float(best[j]) - t
                if ni < 0.0 or ni > 1.0 or nj < 0.0 or nj > 1.0:
                    continue
                h2 = best.copy()
                h2[i] = ni
                h2[j] = nj
                ok, f2 = _try_candidate(h2, bf)
                if ok:
                    best, bf = h2, f2
                    improved = True
                    break

        if not improved:
            break

    return best, bf


def _fourier_h(p, n, K):
    n = int(n)
    K = int(K)
    x = (np.arange(n) + 0.5) * (2.0 / n)
    u = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    idx = 0
    for m in range(1, K + 1):
        if idx + 1 >= len(p):
            break
        amp = float(p[idx])
        phi = float(p[idx + 1])
        idx += 2
        if abs(amp) > 1e-12:
            u += amp * np.cos(math.pi * m * x + phi)
    h = 0.5 + 0.5 * np.tanh(u)
    return _proj(h, n)


def _fourier_obj(p):
    try:
        h = _fourier_h(np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64), _FOU_N, _FOU_K)
        return _c5_manual(h)
    except Exception:
        return 1.0


def _rand_params(K, rng):
    K = int(K)
    base = np.array([0.7, 0.5, 0.35, 0.25, 0.18, 0.12])[:K]
    amps = base * rng.uniform(0.5, 1.8, K)
    phases = rng.uniform(-math.pi, math.pi, K)
    return np.concatenate((amps.astype(np.float64), phases.astype(np.float64)))


def _single_sine_start(n, rng):
    n = int(n)
    best_h = np.full(n, 0.5, dtype=np.float64)
    best_c = 1.0
    amps = [0.18, 0.22, 0.26, 0.30, 0.34, 0.38, 0.42]
    phis = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0 * math.pi, 21, endpoint=False)
    for a in amps:
        for ph in phis:
            p = np.array([float(a), float(ph)], dtype=np.float64)
            h = _fourier_h(p, n, 1)
            c = _c5_manual(h)
            if c < best_c:
                best_c = c
                best_h = h.copy()
    return best_h, best_c


def _sturmian_h(n, alpha):
    n = int(n)
    v = np.mod(np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) * float(alpha), 1.0)
    h = (v < 0.5).astype(np.float64)
    target = int(round(0.5 * n))
    s = int(h.sum())
    if s > target:
        ones = np.where(h > 0.5)[0]
        if len(ones) > 0:
            order = ones[np.argsort(v[ones])][::-1]
            h[order[:max(0, s - target)]] = 0.0
    elif s < target:
        zeros = np.where(h < 0.5)[0]
        if len(zeros) > 0:
            order = zeros[np.argsort(v[zeros])]
            h[order[:max(0, target - s)]] = 1.0
    return _proj(h, n)


def _default_h(n, rng):
    n = int(n)
    x = (np.arange(n) + 0.5) * (2.0 / n)
    u = (
        0.35 * np.cos(math.pi * x + 0.3)
        + 0.25 * np.cos(2.0 * math.pi * x + 1.1)
        + 0.15 * np.cos(3.0 * math.pi * x + 2.2)
    )
    if rng is not None:
        u = u + rng.uniform(-0.05, 0.05, n)
    h = 0.5 + 0.5 * np.tanh(u)
    return _proj(h, n)


def _polish(h, n, rng, quick=False):
    n = int(n)
    h = _proj(h, n)
    f = _c5_manual(h)
    best = h.copy()
    bf = f
    if n < 8:
        return best, bf

    base = max(50, int(20000.0 / max(1, n)))
    if quick:
        base = max(18, int(base * 0.45))

    if not quick:
        stages = (
            (20.0, 0.50, 0.22, 1.0),
            (80.0, 0.45, 0.18, 1.0),
            (300.0, 0.35, 0.15, 1.0),
            (1200.0, 0.20, 0.10, 0.8),
            (1.0e6, 0.0, 0.05, 0.6),
        )
    else:
        stages = (
            (30.0, 0.40, 0.20, 1.0),
            (400.0, 0.20, 0.10, 0.8),
            (1.0e6, 0.0, 0.05, 0.5),
        )

    for beta, mu, step, mult in stages:
        if _left() < 18.0:
            break
        iters = int(base * float(mult))
        if iters < 5:
            continue
        h2, f2 = _pgrefine(best, n, rng, iters, float(beta), float(mu), float(step))
        if f2 < bf - 1e-14:
            best, bf = h2, f2

    if not quick:
        h2, f2 = _local_polish(best, n, rng, sweeps=35, trials=70)
        if f2 < bf - 1e-14:
            best, bf = h2, f2

        if n <= 230 and _left() > 70.0:
            maxit1 = 70 if n <= 180 else 40
            h2 = _slq_improve(best, n, 500.0, maxit1)
            f2 = _c5_manual(h2)
            if f2 < bf - 1e-14:
                best, bf = h2, f2
                h3, f3 = _pgrefine(best, n, rng, int(base * 0.5), 2000.0, 0.2, 0.08)
                if f3 < bf - 1e-14:
                    best, bf = h3, f3

        if _left() > 45.0:
            maxit2 = 50 if n <= 180 else 30
            h2 = _slq_improve(best, n, 2000.0, maxit2)
            f2 = _c5_manual(h2)
            if f2 < bf - 1e-14:
                best, bf = h2, f2
                h3, f3 = _pgrefine(best, n, rng, int(base * 0.4), 4000.0, 0.15, 0.06)
                if f3 < bf - 1e-14:
                    best, bf = h3, f3

        h2, f2 = _local_polish(best, n, rng, sweeps=22, trials=45)
        if f2 < bf - 1e-14:
            best, bf = h2, f2

        for beta, step in ((600.0, 0.08), (2500.0, 0.05), (1.0e6, 0.03)):
            if _left() < 30.0:
                break
            h2, f2 = _pgrefine(best, n, rng, int(base * 0.5), beta, 0.2, step)
            if f2 < bf - 1e-14:
                best, bf = h2, f2
            else:
                break
    else:
        h2, f2 = _local_polish(best, n, rng, sweeps=8, trials=30)
        if f2 < bf - 1e-14:
            best, bf = h2, f2

        if _left() > 20.0:
            h2, f2 = _pgrefine(best, n, rng, int(base * 0.4), 1.0e6, 0.0, 0.04)
            if f2 < bf - 1e-14:
                best, bf = h2, f2

    return best, bf


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    global _t0, _tb, _LOCALEND, _FOU_N, _FOU_K

    _t0 = time.time()
    _tb = float(budget_s)
    _LOCALEND = None
    rng = np.random.RandomState(int(seed))

    try:
        hpar = np.asarray(initial_h_values, dtype=np.float64)
        npar = int(len(hpar))
        if npar < 4:
            raise ValueError("bad parent")
    except Exception:
        npar = 160
        hpar = _default_h(npar, rng)

    if npar % 2 != 0 or npar < 16:
        npar2 = npar if (npar % 2 == 0 and npar >= 16) else 160
        hpar = _interp_h(hpar, int(len(hpar)), npar2)
        npar = int(npar2)

    best_h = _proj(hpar, npar)
    best_n = int(npar)
    best_c = _c5_manual(best_h)
    print("INIT n={} C5={:.9f}".format(best_n, best_c))

    if budget_s > 30.0:
        allow1 = min(500.0, max(120.0, 0.40 * budget_s))
        if allow1 > _left():
            allow1 = max(30.0, _left() * 0.9)
        _LOCALEND = time.time() + allow1
        h2, c2 = _polish(best_h, best_n, rng, quick=False)
        _LOCALEND = None
        if c2 < best_c:
            best_h, best_n, best_c = h2, best_n, c2
            print("STAGE1 n={} C5={:.9f}".format(best_n, best_c))

    if _left() > 150.0:
        allow2 = min(330.0, max(60.0, 0.28 * budget_s))
        if allow2 > _left() - 30.0:
            allow2 = max(30.0, _left() - 30.0)
        _LOCALEND = time.time() + allow2

        h_s, c_s = _single_sine_start(best_n, rng)
        if c_s <= best_c + 0.012:
            h2, c2 = _polish(h_s, best_n, rng, quick=True)
            if c2 < best_c:
                best_h, best_c = h2, c2
                print("SINE n={} C5={:.9f}".format(best_n, best_c))

        for alpha in (0.3819660112501051, 0.6180339887498949, 0.7071067811865475, 0.3180339887498949):
            if _left() < 35.0:
                break
            h_st = _sturmian_h(best_n, alpha)
            c_st = _c5_manual(h_st)
            if c_st <= best_c + 0.018:
                h2, c2 = _polish(h_st, best_n, rng, quick=True)
                if c2 < best_c:
                    best_h, best_c = h2, c2
                    print("STURM n={} C5={:.9f}".format(best_n, best_c))

        for K in (2, 3):
            for trial in range(2):
                if _left() < 55.0:
                    break
                p0 = _rand_params(K, rng)
                _FOU_N = best_n
                _FOU_K = K
                try:
                    res = minimize(
                        _fourier_obj,
                        p0,
                        method="Nelder-Mead",
                        options={
                            "maxiter": 350,
                            "xatol": 1e-5,
                            "fatol": 1e-7,
                            "adaptive": True,
                        },
                    )
                    h4 = _fourier_h(np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64), best_n, K)
                except Exception:
                    continue
                c4 = _c5_manual(h4)
                if c4 <= best_c + 0.012:
                    h2, c2 = _polish(h4, best_n, rng, quick=True)
                    if c2 < best_c:
                        best_h, best_c = h2, c2
                        print("FOURIER n={} C5={:.9f}".format(best_n, best_c))

        _LOCALEND = None

    if _left() > 130.0:
        cands = []
        for d in (2, 4, 6, 8, 10, 12, 16, 20, 24, 32, 48, 64, 96):
            nn1 = best_n + d
            nn2 = best_n - d
            if 32 <= nn1 <= 384 and nn1 % 2 == 0:
                cands.append((abs(nn1 - best_n), nn1))
            if 32 <= nn2 <= 384 and nn2 % 2 == 0:
                cands.append((abs(nn2 - best_n), nn2))

        for nn in (128, 160, 192, 224, 256, 288, 320, 352):
            if 32 <= nn <= 384 and nn % 2 == 0:
                cands.append((abs(nn - best_n), nn))

        cands = sorted(set(cands), key=lambda z: (z[0], z[1])) if not isinstance(sorted(set(cands)), list) else None
        # The above expression can be unsafe; do it explicitly below.
        uniq = []
        seen = set()
        for item in cands:
            if item[1] not in seen:
                seen.add(item[1])
                uniq.append(item)
        uniq.sort(key=lambda z: (z[0], z[1]))

        processed = 0
        for _, nn in uniq:
            if processed >= 7 or _left() < 45.0:
                break
            nn = int(nn)
            processed += 1
            if nn == best_n:
                continue
            hi = _interp_h(best_h, best_n, nn)
            c0 = _c5_manual(hi)
            if c0 <= best_c + 0.022:
                allow = min(210.0, max(40.0, _left() * 0.38))
                _LOCALEND = time.time() + allow
                q = (nn > 300) or (_left() < 160.0)
                h2, c2 = _polish(hi, nn, rng, quick=bool(q))
                _LOCALEND = None
                if c2 < best_c:
                    best_h, best_n, best_c = h2, nn, c2
                    print("RESAMPLE n={} C5={:.9f}".format(best_n, best_c))

    if _left() > 45.0:
        allow4 = min(140.0, _left() * 0.85)
        allow4 = max(20.0, allow4)
        _LOCALEND = time.time() + allow4
        final_quick = best_n > 300
        h2, c2 = _polish(best_h, best_n, rng, quick=final_quick)
        _LOCALEND = None
        if c2 < best_c:
            best_h, best_c = h2, c2
            print("FINALPOLISH n={} C5={:.9f}".format(best_n, best_c))

    best_h = _proj(best_h, best_n)
    fc5_manual = _c5_manual(best_h)
    fc5_exact = _c5_exact(best_h)

    # Prefer the exact evaluator if available.
    try:
        ec = float(evaluate_erdos_solution(best_h))
        if math.isfinite(ec):
            fc5_manual = min(fc5_manual, ec)
            fc5_exact = min(fc5_exact, ec)
    except Exception:
        pass

    fc5_return = fc5_exact if fc5_exact <= fc5_manual + 1e-14 else fc5_manual
    print("BEST n={} C5={:.9f}".format(best_n, fc5_return))
    return (best_h, fc5_return, best_n)
```
